18.2       The audit
Theorems I–J together with R1 and R2 were checked in one internal audit pass [S8], performed
within the same project workflow (auditor recorded as Grok Bot) on 18 October 2026, with numeri-
cal code written from scratch except for one reused routine. The verdict was pass with fixes: no fatal
error, three fixable gaps (G1 holomorphy of R on H ∩ {Re z ≤ 0}; G2 the odd-character tail; G3 the
Euler–Maclaurin remainder bookkeeping) and eight minor issues (m1–m8), all addressed in rev. 2
[S7]. This audit is not external refereeing. The inputs (I1)–(I3) were checked numerically but
their proofs were not re-audited in that pass.
    Second internal pass (revision 3). A skeptical-referee re-derivation [S15] covered: the S-law of
Theorem F with the odd-lattice wc handling and the T -law; Lemma 6.1; Theorems O1, O2 and
Lemma P of §A.3; the key steps of Theorem A.1 and Theorem A.2; and the dimension and count
claims. Verdicts and fixes are in Appendix B. No fatal error was found in any proof. The dimension
error (60 + 12 → 36 + 6) and smaller imprecisions are corrected. Input (I3) is thereby audited for
its Green-form part only; the Stokes continuation, R1/R2 (beyond the first pass), Theorems B, C,
D, E, K and the new Theorem 5.1 have had no separate audit pass. This pass was also internal
(same workflow); it is not external refereeing.
    Revision 4. No new audit pass was made. The revision 4 results (§§11–12.1) come from internal
reports [S16]–[S27] with exact checks where stated, and an internal cross-review [S16] that found
the inconsistencies corrected in Appendix C. The short argument for dEis at odd s via pure tensors
(proof of Theorem 11.6) was written for this consolidation and has had no separate check.

18.4   Other caveats
Since rev. 4 the radical forms of aH , aC are derived (modulo F1), and the Z[ζ12 ] L(0) values are
exact PARI-free Shintani sums; only the 7 × 24 cusp-row identities of Theorem K remain numerical
(Category B). The Gauss-mean zero/nonzero decisions are exact by Theorem D; the W7 phase
values are floating-point with a wide margin (Category B). Category B items are listed in each
section and in §19.

18.5   Reconciliation of sources
Where the sources disagree we follow the latest correction. The following supersessions apply
(repository errata dated 2026-10-17 in docs/S2-11DM2ET-X-Axiomatic-Book/, read-only).

 1. t−1/2 coefficient of C: the fitted ≈ 0.8174 [S1, B4] is replaced by aC = 0.8291426171 . . . [S2, A3]
    (ERRATUM_Bridge_C_tminushalf_Coefficient); obstruction (ii) for C over C is upgraded
    from Category B to A.

 2. Component count: the “36-component” vector of [S2, 3] is replaced by the 140-label family
    and its 72-dimensional representation; the lattice is odd and the T -law needs wc [S4, §2]
    (ERRATUM_psi_Cocycle_Component_Count). The leading-order T -law checks of [S2, A5] stand,
    since their shift is ≡ wc mod L′ . Rev. 4 rewording: what was wrong was the 36-component
    Weil claim, not the Weil structure. The 140-label representation is exactly ρ2x2 /5 ⊗ ρx2 /7 ⊗ ρ2 ,
    the Weil representations of the local forms at 5 and 7 tensored with a 4-dimensional 2-adic
    factor on the theta characteristics (Theorem 11.3).

 3. Cusp content of uL : the claim in [S4, §4.2] that uL has a nonzero dihedral cusp part at
    negative frequencies is withdrawn; it came from counting only T + and omitting T − [S5, §0]
    (ERRATUM_psi_Conditions_Removed_Cusp_Claim).

 4. Decomposition range: “C = Caxis + Cthird ” holds only through q 293 ; the coefficient set {0, 1, 32 }
    only through q 101 (ERRATUM_Bridge_C_Decomposition_and_Samples, E1); R is a cone sum
    of an indefinite form (E5).

 5. |G| pattern: “observed only, m < 30” [S1] is replaced by Theorem D [S2, A4], which also adds
    the case 7 | m.

 6. ER cancellation: “Category B, not proved” [S6, §1.2] is superseded by Theorems I–J [S7].

 7. ψEis : “no elementary closed form obtained” and the expectation that cusp rows involve central
    values [S5, §4] are superseded by [S6]: a twisted Herglotz form exists, and the cusp rows involve
    L(0), not L( 21 ).

 8. The audit’s proposed Euler–Maclaurin remainder sign is incorrect for odd p; rev. 2 uses the
    corrected sign [S7, changelog G3].

 9. Normalisations: Φ(a,0) = 2ψ [S4, §3]; [S3] sums uL over ⟨γ⟩-orbits, [S5] writes 21

    m; treat composite m.

 3. The 2-adic factor d2 (k, v) for k ≥ 7 (k = 6: d2 = 36, Proposition 13.17).

 4. uL cusp criterion (rev. 5). The coset property and the squarefree criterion are proved
    (Theorems 13.8, 13.9). Open: a closed criterion for non-squarefree s(s − 2).

s = 25 (12 vs 4) and s = 41 (24 vs 8).

the “cusp onset at s = 11, 19, 25, 41” of revisions 2–3 is withdrawn. At the level of the family
span the cusp part is generic: dcusp > 0 for every odd s in 5..51, including s = 5, 7, 13, 15 where
h+ = #genera (Theorem 11.6). At the level of uL the implication fails at s = 14, 17, 18, 22, 23
(cusp content with h+ = #genera); the correct criterion is Proposition 11.8. The two notions are
distinct. Both are invisible in the radial data at x = 0, which are s-uniform (§§A.4–A.5).

A.3     Modularity status of Cthird

Status (Category A). Proved or exactly checked in [S11], conditional where stated; the words “quan-
tum modular form” for the factor |cx + d| are terminology (Category B).
Verdicts. Cthird is not modular (Theorem E(i) (Baker), Theorem E(ii), and Theorem O2 below);
not mock or mixed mock with a holomorphic factor and an o(1/y) cocycle (O2; O1 for EL , X =
1
2 (ϑhol + EL ) and the E-vector); has no strict quantum-modular values (radial divergence at all
490 rationals with m ≤ 40; “every rational” is Category B); and is renormalised quantum modular
after removing q 7/20 and the theta pieces.
Identity. 2Cthird = q 8 f2,7,7 (−q 10 , −q 14 , q) + 1 + E, checked to q 8000 , and

                               Cthird = 21 (1 − θ3 ) + 21 e(7τ /20) ϑhol + EL .

Lemma P (parity splitting). At every rational x, along q = e(x)e−t , t → 0+ : e(7τ /20)ϑhol ,
θ3 and q 8 f contain only half-integer powers of t; E = q 7/20 EL contains only integer powers
t−1 , t0 , t1 , . . . . Hence the integer-order radial data of Cthird are those of 12 (1+E) and the half-integer
ones those of 21 q 8 f . (Category A, conditional on 2-D Euler–Maclaurin and Hecke’s continuation
theorem; audited once, rev. 3: confirmed, Appendix B.)

Theorem O1 (the sign obstruction). If V is holomorphic and finite-dimensional on Γ ∋ S, T N ,
ρ′ (T N ) is diagonalisable with unimodular eigenvalues, and V (γτ ) = (cτ + d)k ρ′ (γ)V (τ ) + o(1/y),
then none of EL , X or the 72-component E-vector is λ · V . Hence E is not the holomorphic part of
a weight-1 harmonic Maass form, nor a holomorphic weight-1 form, nor mixed mock with modular
factors of weight < 1. (Category A, conditional on the cited analyticity of harmonic-Maass cocycles,
which is needed only for the class-membership corollaries; the |x| profile law is now Theorem 5.1.
Audited once, rev. 3: confirmed after two fixes, Appendix B.) Objects whose cocycles are themselves
of size ≥ 1/y are not excluded (open).

 R6.1 Erratum 2 (post-push erratum item (a)). The statement “s = 11 (+, +) sector (90)
      is irreducible; commutant 1” is wrong. With the correct convention the sector is 18 + 72,
      with commutant 2, and the (−, −) sector (20) is irreducible. The cause was the transposed
      label-map convention in commutant.py and krylov irr.py. Their commutant *.json and
      krylov *.json outputs in Open_Algebraic_Update_2026-10-18/ are invalid evidence.
      Items (b)–(d) of the post-push erratum stand.

 R6.2 Erratum 2 (Proposition 13.13). “Old/new pieces” means: the p-local invariants and
      their complement. Both are subrepresentations. Prime-square sectors are reducible with
      two pieces (Proposition 14.3). The open problem “prime-square irreducibility” of rev. 5 is
      resolved negatively.

 R6.3 Proposition 13.4. The case split is by ε = (−1)v2 (gcd(s,8)) , not by the parity of s. “−V ”
      should read (−1)r Vr . The period-72 statement is now Category A (Theorem 14.6).

twin prime conjecture or the Riemann hypothesis.
 R7.1 Correction (role of ∆). The earlier statement that ∆ constrains the √         S2 -side expansions
      intrinsically is refined: only (s − 2) is intrinsic given c1 ; 7s − 32 and the ∆ terms are tied
      to the locked c2 = (−4, 1) (Remark 15.16, Category A).

 R7.2 Superseded (Category B → A). The k(k − 1) count of extra h-relations (§14.3, status
      box; open problem 1 of §14.6) is proved for all k ≥ 2 (Proposition K1). The resulting
      dimension bounds are proved upper bounds; their sharpness is open.

 R7.3 Superseded. The status of Proposition 14.8(v) (“only a partial conceptual reason” for
      the periods 16 and 96) is replaced by Propositions 15.11 and 15.12: N = 2, 4 fully con-
      ceptual; N = 8 invariants derived by hand and distinctness proved by a residue-level finite
      computation, with no scan over s.

 R7.4 Superseded. “The te/2 coefficients at x = 41 , 16 were not computed” (status of Proposi-
      tion 14.8; open problem 3 of §14.6): they are now given at t11/2 , t13/2 (Proposition 15.13),
      together with two vanishing results.

 R7.5 Label. The CRT factorisation statement (Proposition 14.8(ii)) is called the CRT Factor-
      ization Proposition (CFP). The letter label used for it in [S36] and in two script comments
      is retired; see NOTE_LABEL_CFP.md. The k(k − 1) result is Proposition K1.

 R7.6 Source corrections recorded. [S36], G.2: the (s − 8)-exponent bullet is garbled; the
      correct exponent is max(0, e + 3 − 4j) ([S36], G.0). [S37], §7: the stored c7/verify.json is
      empty, so its engine-agreement sentence is not used; the earlier “direct” block there (t ≥ 0.01)
      is not a valid test and is superseded by verify direct.json (Category B).

 R7.7 (C3) extended. Category A now also for Z/47, Z/59, Z/71 and Z/172 , Z/192 , Z/232
      (Proposition 15.15); general p still cited. s = 121, 3481 remain factor-dimension predic-
      tions.

 R7.8 Added: §15.1–§15.9; Proposition K1; Propositions 15.2–15.15; Theorem 15.8; Defini-
      tion 15.7; Remarks 15.6, 15.16; sources [S36], [S37].

fied; subclassed only). The scripts write their outputs into the current directory.
    Reproduce command.
cd 2026-10-19-Implications-checks
python3 checks.py && python3 cvals.py && python3 weil5.py
cd k3_140 && python3 rev_exact.py && python3 lattices.py \
  && python3 intertwine.py && python3 explicit.py \
  && python3 closed_exact.py && python3 aut.py

G.2    Corrections and supersessions
 R8.1 Scope of K3 statements. [S39] (§4, IC-6) treated N1 = (Z2 , 2A) and N2 (and Mt ) as
      K3 candidates. The 140-label representation is ρN2 , not ρN1 (Theorem 16.2); every K3
      statement here is for N2 only ([S38], §6).

 R8.2 Resolved (Category B → A). [S39]’s hypothesis that the label representation is the
      Weil representation of an order-140 discriminant form (“possibly ρL or ρ∗L ”) is resolved:
      ρrev ∼
           = ρN2 ∼
                 = ρ∗T , with an explicit intertwiner.

 R8.3 IC-1. The coefficient set {0, 1, 23 } of C holds only through q 101 ; [q 102 ]C = 2, [q 144 ]C = 3
      (consistent with item 4 of §18.5).

 R8.4 IC-2. Two different series are called Ps in the RTA manuscript; this paper uses only the
      orthant series.

 R8.5 Corpus corrections (Category B notes). H 3 (K3; Z) = 0; 2 cos(2π/539.9) is algebraic
      of degree 2699 as printed.
                                                                              √
 R8.6 Source typo. [S38] gives the entropy as “≈ 4.93”; the value of 2 log(6 + 35) is 4.9558 . . .
      (Theorem 16.9).

 R8.7 Superseded output. The entries Weil Z5 even dim3, Weil Z5 odd dim2 of checks.json
      (“order mod scalars” 81, 67) are superseded by weil5.json (quotients of order 60); only
      the latter is quoted, as Category B.

 R8.8 Added: §16 (Propositions 16.1, 16.3, 16.5, 16.8; Theorems 16.2, 16.7, 16.9; Corollary 16.6;
      Remarks 16.4, 16.10); §17 (Propositions 17.1, 17.2, 17.4, 17.6; Remarks 17.3, 17.5); this

      within their sections; none is lettered, none is “Theorem L”, and none collides with L1–L7.
